HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001466/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_07478f3c483248bc171b","text":"2. **Open repudiation of the social pact.** Evidence: `ن ب ذ B002` (repudiating/confronting an opponent), activated by `كَلَّا` and the social harm of `ه م ز B004`/`ل م ز B001`. Structural cue: the correction word precedes a forceful passive future. Assigned role: نبذ supplies a juridical-social break. Abductive move: persistent covert disparagement ruptures communal bonds, so the response takes the form of public repudiation. Reading change: the casting is not random disposal but a declared break with the offender's social order.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001466/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2e1ddc279dc4bd839429","text":"2. **Covert repudiation becomes open repudiation.** Fixed `ن ب ذ B002` carries openly declaring separation or confrontation to an opponent. That branch is activated by the earlier contrast between covert fault-finding, `ل م ز B001`, and disparaging attack, `ه م ز B004`. The packet supplies concealed social aggression and then emphatic casting; I infer that the latter makes rejection public and unmistakable. `لَيُنۢبَذَنَّ` can thus mean more than physical hurling: the one who informally expelled others from social worth is formally and openly repudiated.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000337/B004","root_000337/B006","root_001466/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_36f55b749e00e9d7998b","text":"`104:8`-`104:9` retrospectively changed `فِى ٱلْحُطَمَةِ` from a destination into engineered confinement. Sealing and extended columns give `ح ط م B004` and `B006` a visible pressure-mechanism: occupants cannot disperse, so driving and crowding can become crushing. The later architecture also strengthens the contrast with `ن ب ذ B001`: the initial throw is free movement only until it terminates in a space designed to prevent any further movement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000337/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3e5bcfa90c73561dd725","text":"4. **The Crusher as a carceral pressure-agent.** Fixed `ح ط م B004` images a harsh driver who crowds livestock together or denies pasture, while `ح ط م B006` images a mass crushing what it meets. Later `مُّؤْصَدَةٌ`, `و ص د B001`, and `عَمَدٍ مُّمَدَّدَةٍ`, `ع م د B003` plus `م د د B001`, supply sealing and extended structural members. The packet does not explicitly say crowding causes the crushing; I infer that enclosure prevents dispersion and converts a gathered mass into mutual pressure. `فِى ٱلْحُطَمَةِ` then names not a passive location alone but an active confinement apparatus that drives, compacts, and breaks.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000337/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4b4a7d4d198d2bf12836","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:4:4\",\"root_000337\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ezme, dıştan gelen tek bir darbeyle kalmaz; sonraki ateşin yüreklere erişmesiyle insanın içine kadar işleyen bir sürece dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B001","root_001466/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_72a49759753e4fe5c485","text":"1. **The gatherer becomes the discarded object.** Fixed `لَيُنۢبَذَنَّ` realizes `ن ب ذ B001`, casting something away from the hand and from regard. It reverses prior `جَمَعَ`, `ج م ع B001`, which draws scattered property into the possessor's control, and the emphatic passive removes that possessor's agency. The packet supplies gathering followed by casting; I infer an exchange of grammatical roles in which the collector is handled as an item. The ayah therefore reads as a precise status-reversal: one who constituted worth by retaining things is himself removed both from control and from regard.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000337/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8da97254d8b987e10673","text":"3. **The crusher makes worldly goods into debris.** Evidence: `ح ط م B001` (breaking dry/hard matter until it crumbles) and `ح ط م B009` (perishing debris of worldly life), activated by `م و ل B001` in 104:2. Structural cue: the named destination حطمة follows wealth-hoarding. Assigned role: حطم turns the supposedly durable object into fragile debris. Abductive move: wealth is targeted because it was treated as durable; the crusher reveals its true حطام-quality. Reading change: الحطمة becomes not just a place-name but the process that proves worldly accumulation already belongs to breakable debris.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001466/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_acae8f9a468be822fd9b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:4:2\",\"root_001466\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Başkalarını örtülü biçimde toplumsal değerin dışına iten kişi, burada açık ve kesin bir biçimde reddedilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001466/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b38b5f36073b5314e871","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:4:2\",\"root_001466\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Malı elinde tutup toplayan kişi, edilgen biçimde elden çıkarılan ve değer verilmeyen bir nesne konumuna düşürülür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000337/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b5c87d89d49238d8c3af","text":"4. **A violent driver replacing the violent tongue.** Evidence: `ح ط م B004` (harsh driver/shepherd crushing livestock together), activated by `ه م ز B003` and `ل م ز B002` (prodding/pushing). Structural cue: earlier social prods culminate in an agent/place whose image is rough herding. Assigned role: حطمة is the counter-driver. Abductive move: the one who drove others by jabs and slights is driven into a force that handles him with crushing roughness. Reading change: punishment mirrors the offender's social mechanics as rough handling.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001466/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_bcc560daa3f8fe262ca3","text":"1. **Gathering reversed into being thrown away.** Evidence: `ن ب ذ B001` (casting/throwing away), activated by `ج م ع B001/B005` in 104:2. Structural cue: the one who gathered by hand is passively cast away. Assigned role: نبذ is the reversal of possession and grip. Abductive move: a hoarding hand imagines control, but judgment changes the agent into the object handled. Reading change: the ayah reads as dispossession at the bodily level: the gatherer becomes the discarded thing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000337/B004","root_000337/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_dab5efad944f4d37bb4b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:4:4\",\"root_000337\",[\"B004\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ezici yalnızca bir yer değil, içindekileri sıkıştırıp birbirine bastırarak kıran etkin bir kapatma düzeneği olarak görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000337/B001","root_000337/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f37a08d59c8bd2622589","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:4:4\",\"root_000337\",[\"B001\",\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ezici, dayanıklı sanılan serveti ve ona dayanan kimliği kırılabilir dünya döküntüsüne dönüştüren bir gerçeklik sınaması olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000337/B001","root_001457/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f845c9f9d3dd6bdc7035","text":"3. **A conversion from claimed durability to debris.** Fixed `ٱلْحُطَمَةِ` activates `ح ط م B001`, breaking dry or hard matter until it crumbles, and `ح ط م B009`, worldly display and wealth already viewed as perishing debris. Prior `مَالًا`, `م و ل B001`, and `أَخْلَدَهُ`, `خ ل د B001`, supply the claimed durable stock. The text gives wealth, imagined permanence, and then the breaker; I infer the conversion arrow from apparently hard reserve to exposed fragment. The destination consequently reads as a material truth-test that makes both wealth and the identity built on it assume their latent debris-state.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_fe0e06c6bd2b61079ffc","text":"104:6-7 made الحطمة less like a blunt external crusher and more like a fire that crushes by entering and exposing the interior. 104:8 also revised نبذ: being thrown is not a momentary disposal but entry into a sealed state.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
